## Words 2,001 – 2,100

### 2001. [originally](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/originally) *(adverb)* ^w-2001
- **UK:** `/əˈrɪdʒənəli/` · **US:** `/əˈrɪdʒənəli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/ori/origi/originally__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/ori/origi/originally__us_2.mp3)
- **Meaning:** स्पष्टतः, आदि में, शुरु शुरु में
- **Examples:**
  - The school was originally very small.
  - Originally, we had intended to go to Italy, but then we won the trip to Greece.
  - Laib originally planned to be a doctor.
  - She comes originally from York.

### 2144. [praise](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/praise_1) *(noun)* ^w-2144
- **UK:** `/preɪz/` · **US:** `/preɪz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pra/prais/praise__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pra/prais/praise__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** श्लाघा, गुणगान, स्तुत्ति

### 2145. [preach](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/preach) *(verb)* ^w-2145

### 2146. [precede](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/precede) *(verb)* ^w-2146

- **UK:** `/ˈpresɪdənt/` · **US:** `/ˈpresɪdənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/prece/precedent__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/prece/precedent__us_1.mp3)

### 2149. [precisely](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/precisely) *(adverb)* ^w-2149
- **UK:** `/prɪˈsaɪsli/` · **US:** `/prɪˈsaɪsli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/preci/precisely__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/preci/precisely__us_1.mp3)

  - The meeting starts at 2 o'clock precisely.

### 2150. [precision](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/precision) *(noun)* ^w-2150
- **UK:** `/prɪˈsɪʒn/` · **US:** `/prɪˈsɪʒn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/preci/precision__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/preci/precision__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - Her writing is imaginative but lacks precision.
  - with precision He chose his words with precision.
  - done with mathematical precision
  - Historians can’t estimate the date with any (degree of) precision.

### 2151. [predator](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/predator) *(noun)* ^w-2151
- **UK:** `/ˈpredətə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈpredətər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/preda/predator__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/preda/predator__us_1.mp3)

- **UK:** `/ˈpriːdəsesə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈpredəsesər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/prede/predecessor__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/prede/predecessor__us_1.mp3)

### 2153. [predict](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/predict) *(verb)* ^w-2153
- **UK:** `/prɪˈdɪkt/` · **US:** `/prɪˈdɪkt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/predi/predict__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/predi/predict__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - predict something a reliable method of predicting earthquakes
  - Nobody could predict the outcome.
  - to predict the future
  - He predicts a bright future for the business in the next financial year.

### 2154. [predictable](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/predictable) *(adjective)* ^w-2154
- **UK:** `/prɪˈdɪktəbl/` · **US:** `/prɪˈdɪktəbl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/predi/predictable__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/predi/predictable__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पूर्वानुमेय होने के कारण  नीरस, पूर्वानुमेय, पूर्वानुमेय होने के कारण नीरस
- **Examples:**
  - a predictable result
  - The ending of the book was entirely predictable.
  - The disease follows a highly predictable pattern.
  - In March and April, the weather is much less predictable.

### 2155. [prediction](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/prediction) *(noun)* ^w-2155
- **UK:** `/prɪˈdɪkʃn/` · **US:** `/prɪˈdɪkʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/predi/prediction__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/predi/prediction__us_3.mp3)
- **Meaning:** भविष्य कथन, भविष्यवाणी
- **Examples:**
  - The results of the experiment confirmed our predictions.
  - prediction about/on something I've learned not to make predictions about the weather.
  - It's difficult to make accurate predictions about the effects on the environment.
  - Experts preview the game and offer their prediction on the outcome.

### 2158. [prefer](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/prefer) *(verb)* ^w-2158
- **UK:** `/prɪˈfɜː(r)/` · **US:** `/prɪˈfɜːr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/prefe/prefer__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/prefe/prefer__us_3.mp3)

### 2159. [preference](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/preference) *(noun)* ^w-2159

### 2166. [premise](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/premise) *(noun)* ^w-2166
- **UK:** `/ˈpremɪs/` · **US:** `/ˈpremɪs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/premi/premise__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/premi/premise__us_1.mp3)

### 2167. [preparation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/preparation) *(noun)* ^w-2167
- **UK:** `/ˌprepəˈreɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌprepəˈreɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/prepa/preparation__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/prepa/preparation__us_2.mp3)

- **Examples:**

### 2168. [prescribe](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/prescribe) *(verb)* ^w-2168
- **UK:** `/prɪˈskraɪb/` · **US:** `/prɪˈskraɪb/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/presc/prescribe__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/presc/prescribe__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निर्धारित करना, नुस्खा लिखना
- **Examples:**
  - prescribe something Valium is usually prescribed to treat anxiety.
  - prescribe (somebody) something (for something) He may be able to prescribe you something for that cough.

### 2169. [prescription](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/prescription) *(noun)* ^w-2169
- **UK:** `/prɪˈskrɪpʃn/` · **US:** `/prɪˈskrɪpʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/presc/prescription__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/presc/prescription__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - prescription for something The doctor gave me a prescription for antibiotics.
  - on prescription (British English) Antibiotics are only available on prescription.
  - by prescription (North American English) Antibiotics are only available by prescription.
  - They are not available without a prescription.

### 2170. [presence](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/presence) *(noun)* ^w-2170

- **Meaning:** के सामने, व्यक्तित्व, उपस्थिति
- **Examples:**
  - He hardly seemed to notice my presence.
  - Her presence during the crisis had a calming effect.
  - (formal) Your presence is requested at the meeting.
  - in the presence of somebody They married in the presence of their family members.

### 2171. [presentation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/presentation) *(noun)* ^w-2171
- **UK:** `/ˌpreznˈteɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌpriːznˈteɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/prese/presentation__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/prese/presentation__us_1.mp3)

### 2172. [preserve](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/preserve_1) *(verb)* ^w-2172
- **UK:** `/prɪˈzɜːv/` · **US:** `/prɪˈzɜːrv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/prese/preserve__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/prese/preserve__us_4.mp3)

- **Examples:**

  - She managed to preserve her sense of humour under very trying circumstances.
  - He was insistent on preserving the integrity of the brand.

### 2173. [preservation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/preservation) *(noun)* ^w-2173

- **UK:** `/prɪˈzjuːməbli/` · **US:** `/prɪˈzuːməbli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/presu/presumably__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/presu/presumably__us_1.mp3)

### 2177. [presume](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/presume) *(verb)* ^w-2177
- **UK:** `/prɪˈzjuːm/` · **US:** `/prɪˈzuːm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/presu/presume__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/presu/presume__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मान लेना, चुनौती देना, साहस करना
- **Examples:**
  - They are very expensive, I presume?
  - ‘Is he still abroad?’ ‘I presume so.’
  - presume (that)… I presumed (that) he understood the rules.
  - it is presumed that… Little is known of the youngest son; it is presumed that he died young.

### 2325. [reasoning](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/reasoning) *(noun)* ^w-2325

### 2404. [reluctant](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/reluctant) *(adjective)* ^w-2404
- **UK:** `/rɪˈlʌktənt/` · **US:** `/rɪˈlʌktənt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rel/reluc/reluctant__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rel/reluc/reluctant__us_1.mp3)

### 2412. [remind](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/remind) *(verb)* ^w-2412
- **UK:** `/rɪˈmaɪnd/` · **US:** `/rɪˈmaɪnd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rem/remin/remind__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rem/remin/remind__us_2.mp3)

  - remind somebody/yourself about/of something ‘Don't forget the camera.’ ‘Remind me about it nearer the time.’

### 2413. [reminder](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/reminder) *(noun)* ^w-2413

### 2762. [speculate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/speculate) *(verb)* ^w-2762
- **UK:** `/ˈspekjuleɪt/` · **US:** `/ˈspekjuleɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/spe/specu/speculate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/spe/specu/speculate__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**

  - speculate why, how, etc… It is useless to speculate why he did it.

- **Examples:**
  - They drove away slowly to avoid arousing suspicion.
  - on suspicion of something He was arrested on suspicion of murder.
  - suspicion that… I have a sneaking suspicion that she's not telling the truth.
  - My suspicions were confirmed when police raided the property.

- **UK:** `/səˈspɪʃəs/` · **US:** `/səˈspɪʃəs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sus/suspi/suspicious__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sus/suspi/suspicious__us_1.mp3)

### 2926. [sustain](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/sustain) *(verb)* ^w-2926

### 2991. [transform](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/transform) *(verb)* ^w-2991
